_scratchpad\p2_536_537\frozen\fbpending_bound.why
WHY THIS MUTANT, AND WHY THIS ROW.

The anchor is #442's own reset, `d->pending_timer_interrupts[nr] = 1;` in
footbridge_pending() at src/devices/dev_footbridge.c.  Changing the 1 to a 0 leaves the file
compiling, leaves the BOUND intact, and leaves the overflow fixed -- it breaks only the
"one interrupt is still owed" half, coalescing the debt to nothing instead of to one tick.

WHY THAT MUTANT AND NOT A BIGGER ONE.  Deleting the bound entirely is caught by seven R1
rows and is the pre-fix state, so it tests nothing the pre-fix arm does not.  This one is the
narrowest edit that a reader would plausibly make -- "reset to zero" reads tidier than "reset
to one" -- and it is invisible to every row except R3.

MEASURED, 2026-08-20, all seven emulated_hz/load cases:
  pristine            R3 lowest value the counter ever held: 1     FBBOUND_VERDICT=PASS
  1 -> 0              R3 lowest value the counter ever held: 0     FBBOUND_VERDICT=FAIL, 7 rows
  and R1 stays GREEN on all seven cases in the mutant arm, which is the point: the bound is
  still doing its job, so a verdict-only or R1-only assertion would score this as fine.

*** THE PIN IS "R4 every case bounded", NOT R3, AND THAT IS MECHANICAL. ***  selfmutant.py
requires the pinned row id to name EXACTLY ONE row (it refuses ambiguity, having been bitten
by a rowid as ordinary as "fail" matching several).  R1 and R3 each print SEVEN times, once
per case, so neither can be pinned.  R4 is an aggregate row added for exactly this purpose
and it goes red whenever any case above does.

The rung-3 reproduction for this defect is regress/fbpending_drain_probe.py and it is a
DIFFERENT artifact with a different job: it drives real guest MMIO on an unmodified -E cats.
This harness calls timer_tickN() directly, which the witness ladder classes as never a
reproduction.  Both ship because neither alone catches what the other does -- measured: a
mutant deleting the absolute ceiling PASSES the rung-3 probe on every timer and is caught
only here, at an emulated_hz above 68 MHz.

1: #!/bin/bash
2: # GATE 2 -- offline differential of the REAL ieee_store_float_value().
3: #
4: # The strongest gate here, and the cheapest, but only because it links the shipped
5: # src/core/float_emul.c. The first version of this gate transcribed BOTH sides of the
6: # differential and compared the copy against itself -- it never compiled or executed
7: # float_emul.c at all, so deleting #287 from the shipped source left it green. Review
8: # caught that, not the gate. Two things now prevent a repeat:
9: #
10: #   * the real file is compiled and linked into the driver, and
11: #   * the file compiled is asserted byte-identical to the committed one, so "the test
12: #     passed" and "the repository is correct" are the same statement.
13: #
14: # misc.h includes ../../config.h, which configure generates, so the compile has to happen
15: # against a CONFIGURED tree. That tree is a copy of the committed source (no VPATH in this
16: # project), which is exactly why the identity check above is required rather than assumed.
17: set -u
18: HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
19: . "$HERE/lib.sh"
20: 
21: SEC=$ROOT/GXEMUL-SEC
22: SRC=core/float_emul.c
23: 
24: gate_begin "offline-differential"
25: 
26: # #375: the mips*_loadstore[32] dispatch-table index is a SILENT CROSS-FILE
27: # COUPLING. generate_mips_loadstore.c emits the table in the loop order
28: # `endianness -> store -> size -> signedness`, so an entry's index is
29: # endianness*16 + store*8 + size*2 + signedness. That arithmetic is then
30: # hard-coded BY HAND, with no comment and no check, at a dozen sites: the
31: # multi-transfer fold's bail (`mips32_loadstore[5]` = plain lw), the coproc
32: # handlers (lwc1->5, swc1->12, ldc1->7, sdc1->14), the canonical decoder, and
33: # the COMBINE(nop)/strlen/#169 matcher slots. Reorder that one generator loop
34: # and every hand-coded index silently mis-resolves -- the fold bail dispatches
35: # the wrong access SIZE, ldc1 becomes lw -- and NOTHING fails to compile. These
36: # rows turn the coupling into a checked invariant, offline, needing no compiler
37: # and no rig (only the committed generated table). Found by the #46 MIPS-combiner
38: # audit; both boot rigs are LE, so the _be entries are dead code on the harness
39: # but the coupling is real for any future BE MIPS work.
40: #
41: # The byte rows are load-bearing in the opposite direction: size==0 has no
42: # endianness, so the generator makes the byte LE and BE entries the SAME symbol
43: # ([1]==[17], [8]==[24]). #169's byte-store check and the strlen byte-load check
44: # rely on that share; the word rows ([5]!=[21]) prove the discrimination the byte
45: # rows deliberately lack. If a generator change ever split the byte entries, the
46: # `|| [x+16]` matcher tautologies become live and those checks silently weaken.
47: MIPSLS=$SEC/src/cpus/tmp_mips_loadstore.c
48: need_file "$MIPSLS"
49: 
50: #  ls_entry <array-name> <index> -> the function symbol at that index, read from
51: #  the committed generated table (the [32] arrays, not the [16] generic ones).
52: ls_entry() {
53:     awk -v arr="$1" -v idx="$2" '
54:         $0 ~ ("\\*" arr "\\[32\\]") { f = 1; n = 0; next }
55:         f && /^};/ { f = 0 }
56:         f { gsub(/[ \t,};]/, "");
57:             if ($0 != "") { if (n == idx) { print $0; exit } n++ } }
58:     ' "$MIPSLS"
59: }
60: 
61: for arr in mips32_loadstore mips_loadstore; do
62:     pfx=${arr%_loadstore}
63:     check "  $arr[5]  = plain word-load LE"  "$(ls_entry $arr 5)"  "${pfx}_instr_l4_le"
64:     check "  $arr[12] = plain word-store LE" "$(ls_entry $arr 12)" "${pfx}_instr_s4_le"
65:     check "  $arr[21] = plain word-load BE"  "$(ls_entry $arr 21)" "${pfx}_instr_l4_be"
850: fi

852: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
853: #  CAN THESE DETECTORS STILL FAIL?  (#436)
854: #
855: #  Gate 3 is the battery's only failability control and it mutates ONE file,
856: #  src/core/float_emul.c.  Eleven differentials live here; gate 3 names one.  A
857: #  measuring seat reproduced the consequence: stub check() so it never compares,
858: #  and 118 rows across five detectors print `ok`, keep their row counts, keep
859: #  their identity rows, keep their verdict tokens -- and detect NOTHING, with
860: #  every assertion in this file green.
861: #
862: #  The reason was structural, not an oversight in any one row: every assertion in
863: #  this file grepped the detector's own stdout, and all of that stdout is downstream
864: #  of the single comparison the stub removes.  They were ONE EQUIVALENCE CLASS, and
865: #  the stub stepped over all of it at once.
866: #
867: #  PAST TENSE ON PURPOSE.  A pass-2 seat pointed out that this sentence was written
868: #  in the PRESENT tense by the very hunk that falsified it: the five selfmutant_one
869: #  checks grep the HELPER's output (varied input) and the manifest checks grep the
870: #  FILESYSTEM, so a handful of this gate's assertions are now outside the class.
871: #  That is the whole point of adding them, and stating it as a present fact would
872: #  have made the comment wrong the moment it landed.  Only a check that VARIES THE INPUT and
873: #  demands the output track it escapes.
874: #
875: #  TWO CONTROLS, COVERING DIFFERENT FAILURES, NEITHER SUBSTITUTING FOR THE OTHER:
876: #    * the in-detector SELFCHECK row (in the .c files) -- feeds check() a
877: #      mismatch and requires the failure counter to move.  Covers EVERY row in
878: #      its file against the comparator dying.  Free, no extra compile.
879: #    * the self-mutants below -- break the code the detector watches and require
880: #      the NAMED row to catch it.  Covers ONE site against a fixture that has
881: #      stopped reaching the code.  Measured: the SELFCHECK is blind to a row
882: #      rewritten to compare the subject against itself; a pinned mutant is not.
883: #
884: #  WHAT THIS DOES NOT PROVE, said plainly because the honest scope is the whole
885: #  point.  A seat stubbed every row EXCEPT the ones each mutant kills and both
886: #  this gate and the self-mutant stayed green -- 23 of 24 rows inert for timer,
887: #  25 of 27 for m8invread.  So this is a LIVENESS CONTROL: it proves the detector
888: #  is not wholly inert and that one named row still fires.  It is not a census
889: #  and must never be read as "the detector is fine".  Going from 0 to 1 is not
890: #  false comfort; CALLING 1 "the detector works" is.
891: selfmutant_one() {   # harness subject stem row-id [extra-cc-flags]
892:     local h="$1" subj="$2" stem="$3" row="$4" xflags="${5:-}"
893:     local o="$HERE/selfmutants/$stem.old" n="$HERE/selfmutants/$stem.new"
894:     if [ ! -f "$o" ] || [ ! -f "$n" ]; then
895:         check "selfmutant $stem: fragments present" "no" "yes"; return
896:     fi
897:     local out
898:     #  The extra flags are the caller's, because the caller owns the real compile line.
899:     #  memory_rw needs -fno-optimize-sibling-calls, which THIS FILE calls load-bearing for
900:     #  the loop-not-recursion row; the helper hardcoded one flag set and so compiled that
901:     #  harness differently from the battery, which the pristine control arm cannot detect
902:     #  because both arms are then consistently wrong.
903:     out=$(SELFMUTANT_EXTRA_FLAGS="$xflags"           python3 "$HERE/selfmutant.py" "$h" "$subj" "$o" "$n" "$row" 2>&1)
904:     printf '%s\n' "$out" | grep -E '^       ' | sed 's/^/    /'
905:     #  ASSERT THE OK TOKEN IS PRESENT, never "FAIL is absent": a setup error emits
906:     #  neither, so a negative form is satisfied by a control that never ran.
907:     check "selfmutant $stem: the detector still detects, via its named row" \
908:           "$(printf '%s' "$out" | grep -c 'SELFMUTANT_OK')" "1"
909: }
910: #  *** F1: THE SELFCHECK ROW NEEDED A PRESENCE CHECK, AND NOT HAVING ONE WAS THE WORST
911: #  DEFECT IN THE FIRST VERSION OF THIS ROUND. ***  A pass-2 seat deleted the whole SELFCHECK
912: #  block from all five detectors and measured: gate 2 stayed PASS at the SAME check count with
913: #  ZERO red rows, byte-identical to baseline, and all five self-mutants still OK.
914: #
915: #  It is unprotected PRECISELY BECAUSE THE BOOKKEEPING IS CORRECT: the row restores `rows`, so
916: #  it is invisible to the only mechanism this gate has for noticing a deleted row -- the
917: #  `rows actually run` floor.  A few hundred lines above, this file already says "Name each row
918: #  that is the SOLE detector of something, so deleting one is a red row rather than a quieter
919: #  file."  The SELFCHECK row is the sole detector of comparator death in five files and was
920: #  named nowhere.  The doctrine was written down and then not applied to the next row added.
921: #
922: #  *** THE LIST THAT STOOD HERE WAS FIVE HARDCODED NAMES WITH NO MANIFEST BEHIND IT, and it
923: #  has moved to the end of this file (selfcheck7). ***  It asserted that five detectors ran
924: #  their sentinel and said NOTHING AT ALL about the other seven differentials, so a
925: #  differential arriving without a comparator control was declared nowhere -- the same
926: #  G-with-no-H shape the selfmutant manifest below was written to close, in this same file,
927: #  a few hundred lines apart.
928: #
929: #  IT COULD NOT BE FIXED IN PLACE, AND THE REASON IS A STALE-ARTEFACT TRAP RATHER THAN
930: #  TIDINESS.  Six of the twelve differentials do not compile or run until several hundred
931: #  lines further down, and $LOGDIR PERSISTS BETWEEN RUNS: a manifest-driven grep of
932: #  diff_sh4_tmu.log FROM HERE would read the PREVIOUS run's log and report a sentinel this
933: #  run never executed.  That is "regenerate before grepping a generated file" wearing
934: #  different clothes.  The whole control now lives in the SC_COVERED block just above
935: #  gate_end, where every log is this run's.
936: 
1249: # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #442 fbpending
1250: #  THE FOOTBRIDGE PENDING-INTERRUPT COUNTER, bounded.  Unbounded, it overflowed INT_MAX and
1251: #  the emulated clock delivered NOTHING for 2^31 further ticks -- reproduced at rung 3 on an
1252: #  unmodified -E cats, three runs, wrap at ~43 s and a 40 s dead window.
1253: #
1254: #  THIS ROW IS A DETECTOR, NOT THE REPRODUCTION, and the distinction is load-bearing here.
1255: #  It #includes the shipped dev_footbridge.c and calls timer_tickN() directly, which the
1256: #  witness ladder's mechanical discriminator classes as never-a-reproduction.  The
1257: #  reproduction is regress/fbpending_drain_probe.py (rung 3) and it is NOT run here.
1258: #
1259: #  BOTH SHIP BECAUSE NEITHER ALONE IS ENOUGH -- measured, not assumed:
1260: #    * the rung-3 probe cannot reach the boundary.  The core delivers at most
1261: #      TIMER_MAX_CATCHUP * TIMER_BASE_FREQUENCY = 68,157,440 increments per wall second, so
1262: #      2^31 of them take 31.5 s AT BEST and a gate row cannot spend that.  This harness does
1263: #      2^31 direct increments in ~4 s.
1264: #    * a mutant deleting the absolute ceiling (leaving the bound at the timer's own rate)
1265: #      PASSES the rung-3 probe on every timer and is caught only here, at emulated_hz above
1266: #      68 MHz -- reachable through the documented -I flag.
1267: #
1268: #  It links every object EXCEPT main.o and dev_footbridge.o, so it runs the REAL timer.c and
1269: #  the REAL footbridge rather than re-implementations.  Measured kills: pre-fix fails R1 x7
1270: #  and R2 with trough -2147483648 (the overflow itself); reset-to-0 fails R3 x7; a fix applied
1271: #  to one callback only fails R1 x7; no-ceiling fails R1 on the two high-hz cases and R2 with
1272: #  peak 2147483647.
1273: FBBIN=$LOGDIR/diff_fbpending_bound
1274: FBLOG=$LOGDIR/diff_fbpending_bound.log
1275: FBOBJS=$(find "$SEC/../build/src" -name '*.o' ! -name 'main.o' ! -name 'dev_footbridge.o' 2>/dev/null | sort)
1276: if [ -z "$FBOBJS" ]; then
1277:     note "fbpending bound: no build/ objects -- SKIPPED, not passed"
1278: else
1279:     if ! $CC -O2 -std=gnu99 -I"$SEC/src/include" -I"$SEC/src/include/thirdparty"             -I"$SEC/src/devices" -o "$FBBIN" "$HERE/diff_fbpending_bound.c"             $FBOBJS -lm -L/usr/X11R6/lib -lX11 > "$FBLOG" 2>&1; then
1280:         note "fbpending bound compile failed:"; sed 's/^/       /' "$FBLOG" | head -12
1281:         check "fbpending bound: links against the real timer.c" "no" "yes"
1282:     else
1283:         check "fbpending bound: links against the real timer.c" "yes" "yes"
1284:         #  The depth argument is what turns R2 on.  Without it R2 SKIPS -- and a skipped row
1285:         #  that reads as a pass is this project's fifth vacuity class, so the count below
1286:         #  pins that R2 actually ran.
1287:         "$FBBIN" 2147487744 > "$FBLOG" 2>&1
1288:         sed 's/^/       /' "$FBLOG" | tail -20
1289:         check "fbpending bound: offline verdict" "$(grep -c '^FBBOUND_PASS$' "$FBLOG")" "1"
1290:         check "fbpending bound: R2 actually ran (not SKIPPED)"               "$(grep -c 'R2 no signed overflow.*direct ticks' "$FBLOG")" "1"
1291:         check_min "fbpending bound: rows actually run"               "$(grep -cE '^  (ok|FAIL) ' "$FBLOG")" 15
1292:     fi
1293: fi
1294: 
1295: RTCBIN=$LOGDIR/diff_rtc_range
1296: RTCLOG=$LOGDIR/diff_rtc_range.log
1297: if ! $CC -O2 -std=gnu99 -I"$SEC/src/include" -I"$SEC/src/include/thirdparty" \
1298:         -ffunction-sections -fdata-sections -Wl,--gc-sections \
1299:         -o "$RTCBIN" "$HERE/diff_rtc_range.c" > "$RTCLOG" 2>&1; then
1300:     note "RTC range differential compile failed:"; sed 's/^/       /' "$RTCLOG" | head -12
2258: #  THE G -- and it is not made redundant by the H above.  The H proves the block EXISTS in the
2259: #  source; this proves it RAN, which is a different failure: a block behind a dead #ifdef, a
2260: #  differential whose compile failed, or a driver that died before reaching it all satisfy the
2261: #  H and fail this.  Assert the OK-token is PRESENT rather than "no FAIL line", because a
2262: #  detector that never started prints neither and a negative form is satisfied by a control
2263: #  that never ran.
2264: sc_notrun=""
2265: for _stem in $SC_COVERED; do
2266:     grep -q 'SELFCHECK the comparator can still fail' "$LOGDIR/diff_$_stem.log" 2>/dev/null \
2267:         || sc_notrun="$sc_notrun $_stem"
2268: done
2269: check "SELFCHECK manifest: every COVERED detector ran its sentinel" \
2270:       "${sc_notrun:-none}" "none"

2234:     #  fail.  This entry is the honest form: DETECTOR, ungated, dated, and filed. ***
2235:     "artifacts/ip30abortkill/ip30_abort_probe.py":
2236:                                    ("2026-09-27",
2237:                                     "no gate runs an SGI IP30 guest session (gate_asan_sweep.sh "
2238:                                     "constructs only); filed to wiringcliff with #518/#520/#530"),
2239: }

2241: DATE_RE = re.compile(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$")
2242: 
2243: 
2244: #  *** A PYTHON DICT LITERAL ACCEPTS A DUPLICATE KEY IN SILENCE, AND THE LAST ONE WINS.
2245: #  MEASURED ON THIS FILE, HOURS AFTER IT WAS WRITTEN. ***
2246: #
2247: #  Two rounds added `sgi_eaddr_probe.py` to MANIFEST independently -- the author of this
2248: #  checker, and the #446 round whose probe it was.  Both landed, four lines apart, and
2249: #  `len(MANIFEST)` reported ONE entry.  Nothing complained, because nothing can: by the
2250: #  time the module object exists the earlier entry is already gone.  Here the two agreed
2251: #  on CLASS, so the behaviour was identical and the only damage was a stale line.  Had
2252: #  they disagreed -- one DETECTOR, one TOOL -- the file would have carried a visible,
2253: #  reviewed, *tracked* classification that the checker silently did not use, and the
2254: #  filename tooth that refuses `*_probe.py` in class `tool` would have been reading the
2255: #  wrong entry while looking correct in the diff.
2256: #
2257: #  It has to be caught by re-reading THIS FILE'S SOURCE TEXT, because the data structure
2258: #  cannot represent the fault it suffers from.  A duplicate is a SETUP FAULT, not a
2259: #  finding: it exits before any wiring is derived, so it can never be confused with a
2260: #  probe that is genuinely unwired.
2440:                         #  sharing a basename in different directories stay distinct,
2441:                         #  where basename keys would mark BOTH gated when only one is.
2442:                         rel = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(d, b)).replace(os.sep, "/")
2443:                         if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(regress, rel)):
2444:                             hits.setdefault(rel, []).append("%s:%d" % (script, no))
2445:                         else:

2447:                     break             # the first .py argument is the script

2451: #  STANDALONE WORDS ONLY.  The first draft used a plain substring and reported
2452: #  mips_fold_probe.py as self-labelled "witness" -- its summary line reads "the MIPS
2453: #  fold-witness COUNTERS", where the word names a counter, not the artefact.  That is the
2454: #  substring trap in the one place in this file that was allowed to be a heuristic, and an
2455: #  advisory that cries wolf is an advisory nobody reads.  MEASURED: with the hyphen guard
2456: #  the noise line disappears and pit8253's real disagreement still fires.
2457: _LBL_W = re.compile(r"(?<![\w-])witness(?![\w-])", re.I)
2458: _LBL_D = re.compile(r"(?<![\w-])detector(?![\w-])", re.I)

2460: 
2461: def domain(regress):
2462:     """The .py artefacts this check governs, and (mode, why) describing how it decided.
2463: 
2464:     *** COMMITTED-OR-STAGED, NOT "ON DISK", AND THE FIRST VERSION HAD IT WRONG. ***  The
2465:     first draft enumerated the directory, and MINUTES AFTER IT WAS WRITTEN a concurrent
2466:     round dropped `sgi_eaddr_probe.py` into regress/ as untracked in-flight work.  This
2467:     check went red -- correctly identifying a real unwired detector, but firing at a
2468:     file its author had not yet offered for commit, in someone else's working tree.
2469: 
2470:     `git ls-files` reads the INDEX, so a probe enters the domain the moment it is `git
2471:     add`ed -- which is precisely when "every COMMITTED detector" starts to apply, and
2472:     precisely when the author is present to classify it.  A scratch probe that is never
2473:     staged defends nothing in a fresh clone either, so it is honestly out of scope.
2474: 
2475:     FALLBACK IS ANNOUNCED, NEVER SILENT.  Outside a work tree (the --selftest copies)
2476:     git cannot answer, and a fallback that quietly changes what a check governs is the
2477:     kind of silent scope change this harness keeps being bitten by.  So the mode is
2478:     printed on the summary line.
2479:     """
2480:     try:
2481:         p = subprocess.run(["git", "ls-files", "--", "*.py"], cwd=regress,
2482:                            capture_output=True, text=True)
2483:         if p.returncode == 0:
2484:             #  #471: NO `"/" not in f` FILTER.  Its docstring always claimed "every
2485:             #  committed DETECTOR under regress/", and the filter made that false --
2486:             #  the checker could not see below top level, which is exactly where
2487:             #  #465 shipped a detector that no gate ran.
2488:             #
2489:             #  `git ls-files` yields paths RELATIVE to regress/, so a top-level file
2490:             #  still arrives as a bare basename and every existing MANIFEST key keeps
2491:             #  matching unchanged.  Only nested files gain a path key.
2492:             files = sorted(f for f in p.stdout.split()
2493:                            if f.endswith(".py")
2494:                            and os.path.isfile(os.path.join(regress, f)))
2495:             if files:
2496:                 return files, "tracked-or-staged"
2497:     except OSError:
2498:         pass
2499:     #  #471: the FALLBACK must descend too.  Leaving it flat would re-create the blind
2500:     #  spot on any machine where git cannot answer -- a check whose coverage depends
2501:     #  on which branch it took is the shape this file exists to refuse.
2502:     found = []
2503:     for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(regress):
2504:         dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if d not in (".git", "__pycache__")]
2505:         for f in filenames:
2506:             if f.endswith(".py"):
2507:                 rel = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(dirpath, f), regress)
2508:                 found.append(rel.replace(os.sep, "/"))
2509:     return sorted(found), "on-disk (git could not answer)"
2510: 
2511: 
2512: def self_label(path):
2513:     """The file's OWN summary line, for the SELFLABEL advisory only.  Never a verdict."""
2514:     try:
2515:         head = read(path).splitlines()[:6]
2516:     except OSError:
2517:         return None
2518:     for ln in head:
2519:         has_w, has_d = bool(_LBL_W.search(ln)), bool(_LBL_D.search(ln))
2520:         if has_w and not has_d:
2521:             return CLASS_WITNESS
2522:         if has_d and not has_w:
2523:             return CLASS_DETECTOR
2524:         if has_w and has_d:
2525:             return None
2526:     return None
2527: 
2528: 
2529: #  =======================================================================================
2530: #  SELFTEST.  Mutants are applied to COPIES in a temp dir; nothing in the repository is
2531: #  touched, so no .MUTANT sentinel is needed.  EXIT STATUS IS TAKEN FROM THE PROCESS: this
2532: #  project lost a whole battery result to `cmd | tail`, which reports tail's status, so a
2533: #  KILLED run read as exit 0.  subprocess.run(...).returncode cannot make that mistake.
2534: #
2535: #  A CHECKER-SOURCE MUTANT THAT DOES NOT APPLY EXACTLY ONCE IS A FAULT, NEVER A PASS --
2536: #  absorb_census.py's rule, and it is what keeps this selftest from silently going vacuous
2537: #  after someone reformats the manifest below.
2538: #
2539: #  *** AND IT FIRED ON ITS OWN AUTHOR, FIRST RUN. ***  The mutants below started life as
2540: #  plain string literals, which meant the text they searched for existed TWICE in this file:
2541: #  once in the manifest and once in the selftest.  _st_apply refused ("x2 ... want 1") rather
2542: #  than mutating both, which would have silently mutated the selftest instead of the subject.
2543: #  So every mutant target is BUILT from its parts and never written out whole.
2544: def _lit(*parts):
2545:     """Join fragments, so the assembled search string appears nowhere in this source."""
2546:     return "".join(parts)
2547: 
2548: 
2549: def _exline(key):
2550:     """The EXEMPT entry for `key` AS IT APPEARS IN THE SOURCE, whole, however many lines.
2551: 
2552:     *** THIS USED TO REBUILD A ONE-LINE ENTRY FROM THE TABLE, AND THAT WAS WRONG FOR EVERY
2553:     ENTRY THAT WRAPS. ***  Measured 2026-08-30, the day the second exemption was discharged:
2554:     of the six then remaining, the reconstruction matched **zero** -- all six are written
2555:     across two or more lines.  So the selftest's four exemption mutants had nothing to bite
2556:     on and its SKIP arm fired, dropping 4 of 21 mutants.
2557: 
2558:     **That is the worse failure of the two, because the SKIP arm reads as "the EXEMPT table
2559:     is empty, nothing to test" when the truth was "the reconstruction is too narrow".**  A
2560:     check quietly getting weaker while reporting PASS is the shape this project names first,
2561:     and here it would have arrived wearing the clothes of progress.
2562: 
2563:     Extracting the REAL text instead of rebuilding an idealised one removes the whole class:
2564:     it cannot disagree with the source, because it IS the source.  Scans from the key to the
2565:     line that closes the tuple, so single-line and wrapped entries are handled identically.
2566:     *** AND THE SEARCH IS ANCHORED INSIDE THE EXEMPT BLOCK, WHICH IS NOT A DETAIL. ***
2567:     `    "<name>":` appears in BOTH tables -- MANIFEST first, EXEMPT second -- so a bare
2568:     `find()` returns the MANIFEST entry and the mutant deletes the wrong one.  Measured: M1
2569:     and M2 then reported `UNCLASSIFIED ... is not in the MANIFEST` and `ORPHAN EXEMPTION`
2570:     instead of the `UNWIRED DETECTOR` they exist to prove, i.e. the mutant reddened for a
2571:     reason unrelated to the rule under test -- which would have looked like a working check.
2572: 
2573:     This is the wrong-section trap this project already records for gate row lists: anchor on
2574:     something that identifies the SECTION, never on a pattern several sections share.
2575:     """
2576:     src = src_of_self()
2577:     block = src.find("EXEMPT = {")
2578:     if block < 0:
2579:         raise RuntimeError("_exline: no EXEMPT block")
2580:     start = src.find('    "%s":' % key, block)
2581:     if start < 0:
2582:         raise RuntimeError("_exline: %r not in EXEMPT" % key)
2583:     i = start
2584:     while i < len(src):
2585:         nl = src.find("\n", i)
2586:         if nl < 0:
2587:             return ""
2588:         line = src[i:nl + 1]
2589:         if line.rstrip().endswith("),"):
2590:             return src[start:nl + 1]
2591:         i = nl + 1
2592:     raise RuntimeError("_exline: unterminated entry for %r" % key)
2593: 
2594: 
2595: def src_of_self():
2596:     with open(__file__, encoding="utf-8", newline="") as _f:
2597:         return _f.read()
2598:

2600: def _pick_exempt_detector():
2601:     """The selftest's exemplar is DERIVED, never hardcoded.
2602: 
2603:     *** THIS FUNCTION EXISTS BECAUSE THE SELFTEST BROKE THE DAY AN EXEMPTION WAS
2604:     DISCHARGED, WHICH IS THE ONE EVENT IT SHOULD HAVE BEEN CELEBRATING. ***  Four of its
2605:     mutants named `pit8253_latch_probe.py` literally.  When that probe was wired into
2606:     gate_arm.sh and its exemption deleted -- exactly the outcome the EXEMPT table exists
2607:     to drive toward -- three mutants raised KeyError on the missing entry and the fourth
2608:     had its premise inverted.  `precommit_check.sh` reported THE PROBE-WIRING CHECKER CAN
2609:     NO LONGER BE SHOWN TO FAIL, correctly.
2610: 
2611:     So the harness punished progress: doing the right thing to the tree broke the tool
2612:     that asks for it.  A hardcoded exemplar is a frozen claim about a table designed to
2613:     empty, and swapping in another name would only move the tripwire to the next
2614:     discharge.
2615: 
2616:     Any exempt DETECTOR serves: exempt means unwired, which is the precondition all four
2617:     mutants need.  Sorted for determinism, so a failure names the same probe twice.
2618: 
2619:     *** AND THE PICK IS VERIFIED, NOT MERELY CHOSEN -- BUT NOT FOR THE REASON THIS
2620:     PARAGRAPH GAVE UNTIL 2026-08-30. ***  It said `_exline()` "reconstructs a ONE-LINE
2621:     entry from the table", so entries wrapped across two source lines matched nothing.
2622:     `_exline()` EXTRACTS the real source text now, however many lines it spans, and its
2623:     own docstring says so -- so the two halves of one file contradicted each other, the
2624:     stale half sitting in the function that consumes the fresh one.  Found by the pass-2
2625:     seat reviewing the commit that made the change.
2626: 
2627:     **The `count == 1` guard is still load-bearing and its warrant has MOVED.**  It no
2628:     longer defends against a formatting mismatch -- extraction cannot have one.  It
2629:     defends the SELF-MATCH case: a doc comment in this file quoting an EXEMPT line makes
2630:     the extracted text appear twice.  **The MECHANISM stated here was wrong until the
2631:     adjudicating seat built the case: `_st_apply` does NOT silently edit the comment
2632:     instead of the table -- it COUNTS and RAISES**, `MUTANT DID NOT APPLY: '...' x2
2633:     (want 1)`.  So the guard prevents a selftest CRASH, not a silent wrong-edit, and
2634:     `_st_apply`'s own raise is a second line of defence behind it.
2635: 
2636:     Two facts the seat measured that belong here: injecting a verbatim copy of one
2637:     EXEMPT entry makes `src.count()` return 2, the guard skips that name and returns
2638:     the next candidate -- so it is genuinely load-bearing.  And **it never fires
2639:     today**: all six counts are 1.  It is a LATENT defence, which is worth saying
2640:     plainly, because a guard that has never fired is the one most likely to be
2641:     deleted as dead by someone who does not know what it is for.
2642: 
2643:     *A guard whose stated reason expires but whose behaviour is still needed is the
2644:     hardest kind to audit: deleting it looks safe, because the case it cites is gone.*
2649:         if cls != CLASS_DETECTOR:2650:             continue
2651:         if src.count(_exline(name)) == 1:
2652:             return name
2653:     return None

2656: PIT_RUN = '\npython3 pit8253_latch_probe.py "$PMAX" "$STUB" > "$PLOG" 2>&1 || true\n'

2668: def _st_apply(path, old, new, want=1):
2669:     src = read(path)
2670:     n = src.count(old)
2671:     if n != want:
2672:         raise RuntimeError("MUTANT DID NOT APPLY: %r x%d in %s (want %d)"
2673:                            % (old[:56], n, os.path.basename(path), want))
2674:     with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
2675:         fh.write(src.replace(old, new))
2676: 
2677: 
2678: def _st_append(path, text):
2679:     with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
2680:         fh.write(text)

2683: def selftest(verbose=False):
2684:     import shutil
2685:     import subprocess
2686:     import tempfile
2687: 
2688:     src_regress = DEFAULT_REGRESS
2689:     me = os.path.abspath(__file__)
2690:     root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="probewiring_selftest_")
2691:     results = []
2692: 
2693:     #  *** THE BASELINE MUST BE GREEN BY CONSTRUCTION, AND THE FIRST VERSION WAS NOT. ***
2694:     #  fresh() copied EVERY .py from the live tree, so when a concurrent round dropped an
2695:     #  unclassified probe into regress/, M0/M2b/M5a all went red and the selftest announced
2696:     #  "THE CHECKER CAN NO LONGER BE SHOWN TO FAIL".  Right colour, WRONG DIAGNOSIS: it
2697:     #  blamed the checker for an APPARATUS state -- gate_offline's F5 shape, and the
2698:     #  dangerous one, because it points the reader at the wrong file.
2699:     #
2700:     #  So the baseline copies exactly the MANIFEST set.  Live-tree content can no longer
2701:     #  perturb a mutant, and M6 still tests the unclassified-arrival path by ADDING one.
2702:     #  A MANIFEST entry with no source file is a SETUP FAULT here, never a mutant result --
2703:     #  absorb_census.py's rule that a crash is a fault and never a detection.
2704:     def fresh():
2705:         work = tempfile.mkdtemp(dir=root)
2706:         rg = os.path.join(work, "regress")
2707:         os.makedirs(rg)
2708:         #  #471: THE COPY MUST MIRROR THE TREE, NOT FLATTEN IT.  This loop was
2709:         #  `os.listdir`, so once the checker could see nested detectors the selftest
2710:         #  copy still could not -- and every nested MANIFEST entry became a GHOST in
2711:         #  the copy, reddening the unmutated M0 control.
2712:         #
2713:         #  That is the SAME blind spot one level up, inside the harness that tests
2714:         #  for it: a flat copy cannot exercise a recursive scan.
2715:         for f in os.listdir(src_regress):
2716:             if f.endswith(".sh"):
2717:                 shutil.copyfile(os.path.join(src_regress, f), os.path.join(rg, f))
2718:         for rel in MANIFEST:
2719:             if not rel.endswith(".py"):
2720:                 continue
2721:             src = os.path.join(src_regress, rel.replace("/", os.sep))
2722:             if not os.path.isfile(src):
2723:                 continue
2724:             dst = os.path.join(rg, rel.replace("/", os.sep))
2725:             os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
2726:             shutil.copyfile(src, dst)
2727:         chk = os.path.join(work, "check_probe_wiring.py")
2728:         shutil.copyfile(me, chk)
2729:         return rg, chk
2730: 
2731:     absent = [f for f in sorted(MANIFEST) if not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(src_regress, f))]
2732:     if absent:
2733:         print("PROBEWIRING_SELFTEST_SETUP  the MANIFEST names files regress/ does not have:")
2734:         for f in absent:
2735:             print("    %s" % f)
2736:         print("  fix the manifest first; a selftest on a broken baseline proves nothing.")
2737:         return 2

2740:         p = subprocess.run([sys.executable, chk, "--regress", rg] + list(extra),

2507:                 rel = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(dirpath, f), regress)
2508:                 found.append(rel.replace(os.sep, "/"))
2509:     return sorted(found), "on-disk (git could not answer)"
2510: 
2511: 
2512: def self_label(path):
2513:     """The file's OWN summary line, for the SELFLABEL advisory only.  Never a verdict."""
2514:     try:
2515:         head = read(path).splitlines()[:6]
2516:     except OSError:
2517:         return None
2518:     for ln in head:
2519:         has_w, has_d = bool(_LBL_W.search(ln)), bool(_LBL_D.search(ln))
2520:         if has_w and not has_d:
2521:             return CLASS_WITNESS
2522:         if has_d and not has_w:
2523:             return CLASS_DETECTOR
2524:         if has_w and has_d:
2525:             return None
2526:     return None
2527: 
2528: 
2529: #  =======================================================================================
2530: #  SELFTEST.  Mutants are applied to COPIES in a temp dir; nothing in the repository is
2531: #  touched, so no .MUTANT sentinel is needed.  EXIT STATUS IS TAKEN FROM THE PROCESS: this
2532: #  project lost a whole battery result to `cmd | tail`, which reports tail's status, so a
2533: #  KILLED run read as exit 0.  subprocess.run(...).returncode cannot make that mistake.
2534: #
2535: #  A CHECKER-SOURCE MUTANT THAT DOES NOT APPLY EXACTLY ONCE IS A FAULT, NEVER A PASS --
2536: #  absorb_census.py's rule, and it is what keeps this selftest from silently going vacuous
2537: #  after someone reformats the manifest below.
2538: #
2539: #  *** AND IT FIRED ON ITS OWN AUTHOR, FIRST RUN. ***  The mutants below started life as
2540: #  plain string literals, which meant the text they searched for existed TWICE in this file:
2541: #  once in the manifest and once in the selftest.  _st_apply refused ("x2 ... want 1") rather
2542: #  than mutating both, which would have silently mutated the selftest instead of the subject.
2543: #  So every mutant target is BUILT from its parts and never written out whole.
2544: def _lit(*parts):
2545:     """Join fragments, so the assembled search string appears nowhere in this source."""
2546:     return "".join(parts)
2547: 
2548: 
2549: def _exline(key):
2550:     """The EXEMPT entry for `key` AS IT APPEARS IN THE SOURCE, whole, however many lines.
2551: 
2552:     *** THIS USED TO REBUILD A ONE-LINE ENTRY FROM THE TABLE, AND THAT WAS WRONG FOR EVERY
2553:     ENTRY THAT WRAPS. ***  Measured 2026-08-30, the day the second exemption was discharged:
2554:     of the six then remaining, the reconstruction matched **zero** -- all six are written
2555:     across two or more lines.  So the selftest's four exemption mutants had nothing to bite
2556:     on and its SKIP arm fired, dropping 4 of 21 mutants.
2557: 
2558:     **That is the worse failure of the two, because the SKIP arm reads as "the EXEMPT table
2559:     is empty, nothing to test" when the truth was "the reconstruction is too narrow".**  A
2560:     check quietly getting weaker while reporting PASS is the shape this project names first,
2561:     and here it would have arrived wearing the clothes of progress.
2562: 
2563:     Extracting the REAL text instead of rebuilding an idealised one removes the whole class:
2564:     it cannot disagree with the source, because it IS the source.  Scans from the key to the
2565:     line that closes the tuple, so single-line and wrapped entries are handled identically.
2566:     *** AND THE SEARCH IS ANCHORED INSIDE THE EXEMPT BLOCK, WHICH IS NOT A DETAIL. ***
2567:     `    "<name>":` appears in BOTH tables -- MANIFEST first, EXEMPT second -- so a bare
2568:     `find()` returns the MANIFEST entry and the mutant deletes the wrong one.  Measured: M1
2569:     and M2 then reported `UNCLASSIFIED ... is not in the MANIFEST` and `ORPHAN EXEMPTION`
2570:     instead of the `UNWIRED DETECTOR` they exist to prove, i.e. the mutant reddened for a
2571:     reason unrelated to the rule under test -- which would have looked like a working check.
2572: 
2573:     This is the wrong-section trap this project already records for gate row lists: anchor on
2574:     something that identifies the SECTION, never on a pattern several sections share.
2575:     """
2576:     src = src_of_self()
2577:     block = src.find("EXEMPT = {")
2578:     if block < 0:
2579:         raise RuntimeError("_exline: no EXEMPT block")
2580:     start = src.find('    "%s":' % key, block)
2581:     if start < 0:
2582:         raise RuntimeError("_exline: %r not in EXEMPT" % key)
2583:     i = start
2584:     while i < len(src):
2585:         nl = src.find("\n", i)
2586:         if nl < 0:
2587:             return ""
2588:         line = src[i:nl + 1]
2589:         if line.rstrip().endswith("),"):
2590:             return src[start:nl + 1]
2591:         i = nl + 1
2592:     raise RuntimeError("_exline: unterminated entry for %r" % key)
2593: 
2594: 
2595: def src_of_self():
2596:     with open(__file__, encoding="utf-8", newline="") as _f:
2597:         return _f.read()
2598: 
2599: 
2600: def _pick_exempt_detector():
2601:     """The selftest's exemplar is DERIVED, never hardcoded.
2602: 
2603:     *** THIS FUNCTION EXISTS BECAUSE THE SELFTEST BROKE THE DAY AN EXEMPTION WAS
2604:     DISCHARGED, WHICH IS THE ONE EVENT IT SHOULD HAVE BEEN CELEBRATING. ***  Four of its
2605:     mutants named `pit8253_latch_probe.py` literally.  When that probe was wired into
2606:     gate_arm.sh and its exemption deleted -- exactly the outcome the EXEMPT table exists
2607:     to drive toward -- three mutants raised KeyError on the missing entry and the fourth
2608:     had its premise inverted.  `precommit_check.sh` reported THE PROBE-WIRING CHECKER CAN
2609:     NO LONGER BE SHOWN TO FAIL, correctly.
2610: 
2611:     So the harness punished progress: doing the right thing to the tree broke the tool
2612:     that asks for it.  A hardcoded exemplar is a frozen claim about a table designed to
2613:     empty, and swapping in another name would only move the tripwire to the next
2614:     discharge.
2615: 
2616:     Any exempt DETECTOR serves: exempt means unwired, which is the precondition all four
2617:     mutants need.  Sorted for determinism, so a failure names the same probe twice.
2618: 
2619:     *** AND THE PICK IS VERIFIED, NOT MERELY CHOSEN -- BUT NOT FOR THE REASON THIS
2620:     PARAGRAPH GAVE UNTIL 2026-08-30. ***  It said `_exline()` "reconstructs a ONE-LINE
2621:     entry from the table", so entries wrapped across two source lines matched nothing.
2622:     `_exline()` EXTRACTS the real source text now, however many lines it spans, and its
2623:     own docstring says so -- so the two halves of one file contradicted each other, the
2624:     stale half sitting in the function that consumes the fresh one.  Found by the pass-2
2625:     seat reviewing the commit that made the change.
2626: 
2627:     **The `count == 1` guard is still load-bearing and its warrant has MOVED.**  It no
2628:     longer defends against a formatting mismatch -- extraction cannot have one.  It
2629:     defends the SELF-MATCH case: a doc comment in this file quoting an EXEMPT line makes
2630:     the extracted text appear twice.  **The MECHANISM stated here was wrong until the
2631:     adjudicating seat built the case: `_st_apply` does NOT silently edit the comment
2632:     instead of the table -- it COUNTS and RAISES**, `MUTANT DID NOT APPLY: '...' x2
2633:     (want 1)`.  So the guard prevents a selftest CRASH, not a silent wrong-edit, and
2634:     `_st_apply`'s own raise is a second line of defence behind it.
2635: 
2636:     Two facts the seat measured that belong here: injecting a verbatim copy of one
2637:     EXEMPT entry makes `src.count()` return 2, the guard skips that name and returns
2638:     the next candidate -- so it is genuinely load-bearing.  And **it never fires
2639:     today**: all six counts are 1.  It is a LATENT defence, which is worth saying
2640:     plainly, because a guard that has never fired is the one most likely to be
2641:     deleted as dead by someone who does not know what it is for.
2642: 
2643:     *A guard whose stated reason expires but whose behaviour is still needed is the
2644:     hardest kind to audit: deleting it looks safe, because the case it cites is gone.*
2645:     """
2646:     src = src_of_self()
2647:     for name in sorted(EXEMPT):
2648:         cls = MANIFEST.get(name, (None,))[0]
2649:         if cls != CLASS_DETECTOR:
2650:             continue
2651:         if src.count(_exline(name)) == 1:
2652:             return name
2653:     return None

3: 
4: PROVENANCE.  This is the round's rung-3 WITNESS, shipped as its detector with

11: 
12: 
13: DEFECT.  DEVICE_ACCESS(mk48txx) accepts everything outside
14: [MK48T08_CLKOFF, MK48T08_CLKOFF + MK48TXX_ISEC) = [0x1ff0, 0x1ff9) via an early
15: `goto ret'.  The switch it then reaches holds exactly ONE case,
16: MK48T08_CLKOFF + MK48TXX_ICSR = 0x1ff8.  The other EIGHT offsets 0x1ff0..0x1ff7
17: fall to `default:' -> fatal() -> exit(1), in BOTH directions.
18: 
19: CONSTANTS VERIFIED IN-TREE (src/include/thirdparty/mk48txxreg.h):
20:     MK48T08_CLKSZ  8192 (=MK48TXX_LEN, the registration length)
21:     MK48T08_CLKOFF 0x1ff0      MK48TXX_ICSR 8      MK48TXX_ISEC 9
22: Attach: src/machines/machine_mvme88k.c, MVME187 subtype, "mk48txx addr=0xfffc0000".
23: So the killing guest physical addresses are 0xfffc1ff0 .. 0xfffc1ff7.
24: 
25: REACHABILITY -- WHY THE CENSUS IS ALL EIGHT, NOT THE PCC2 SUBSET.  dev_pcc2.c's
26: width kills were half-unreachable because a non-byte m88k access is alignment-checked
27: before memory_rw.  Here the killing accesses are BYTE accesses: cpu_m88k_instr.c
28: decodes op26 0x03 as ld.bu (opsize 0) and 0x0b as st.b (opsize 0), and
29: cpu_m88k_instr_loadstore.c guards its alignment check with `#ifndef LS_1', so a
30: size-1 access is never alignment-checked.  Every one of the eight offsets is
31: guest-reachable in BOTH directions: 16 killing accesses, and the full census is free.
32: 
33: RUNG 3: real MC88100 instructions executed by the CPU through real address decode
34: and real memory_rw, on the UNMODIFIED in-tree `-E mvme88k -e mvme187'.  No machine
35: description edited, no device_add added -- mvme187 already attaches this device.
36: 
37: THE ORACLE, and why it is not a timeout and not a grep.  The symptom is PROCESS
38: DEATH.  Four independent discriminators, and a kill row is scored on the FIRST TWO
39: jointly:
40:   (1) REAPED WAIT STATUS.  subprocess returncode 1 == exit(1); 0 == the debugger's
41:       own `quit'.  A HANG is a distinct third state (TimeoutExpired), so "timed out"
42:       can never be read as "killed".
43:   (2) POST-ACCESS OBSERVABLE.  Every session is st r5,r3,0 ; <ACCESS> ; ld r4,r3,0
44:       and ends `print r4'.  If the guest ran on, r4 == 0x5a5a5a5a; if the host died,
45:       the third step and the print never happen.  This separates DEATH from a
46:       fatal() that merely COMPLAINS AND RETURNS -- which this fork has many of.
47:   (3) ATTRIBUTION BY MESSAGE.  The message names the offset, so the death is tied to
48:       the access rather than to the session.  Corroboration, never proof on its own.
49:   (4) ATTRIBUTION BY SIBLING.  Otherwise-identical sessions at 0x1fef (below the
50:       window), 0x1ff8 (the implemented case) and 0x1ff9 (first accepted clock reg)
51:       must run to completion.
52: 
53: CONTROLS, clause (ii) of the witness ladder:
54:   live      a byte written to NVRAM offset 0x100 round-trips through the same decode
55:   nvram0    a never-written neighbour reads 0x00 -- the array is zero-filled, so a
56:             nonzero clock byte cannot be zero-fill or a stuck bus
57:   devsig *  FOUR clock registers (0x1ffc..0x1fff) are compared against the HOST's own
58:             gmtime, each with its own encoding: year/mon/mday are BCD, wday is
59:             tm_wday+1 RAW.  A four-way match with two different encodings could only
60:             come from mk48txx_update_regs().  RAM answers 0; a fallback answers 0.
61:   shape     the identical session with a RAM load in the access slot exits 0.
62: 
63: TEST-FIRST SHAPE: every `want' below is the FIXED behaviour.  On the committed

136: def session(cmds, timeout=60, subtype="mvme187"):
137:     """-> (transcript, how) where how is exit0 / exit1 / exitN / sigN / TIMEOUT."""
138:     argv = [BIN, "-V", "-x", "-E", "mvme88k", "-e", subtype, "-M", "64",
139:             "0x10000:" + DUMMY]
140:     inp = ("\n".join(list(cmds) + ["quit"]) + "\n").encode("latin-1")
141:     try:
142:         p = subprocess.run(argv, input=inp, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
143:                            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=timeout)
144:     except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
145:         out = (e.output or b"").decode("latin-1", "replace").replace("\r", "")
146:         return out, "TIMEOUT"
147:     rc = p.returncode
148:     how = ("sig%d" % -rc) if rc < 0 else ("exit%d" % rc)
149:     return p.stdout.decode("latin-1", "replace").replace("\r", ""), how

152: def printed(t, reg):
153:     """the bare 0x... line the debugger emits after `print rN', skipping chatter."""
154:     m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\b" % reg, t)
155:     if not m:
156:         return "absent"
157:     for line in t[m.end():].split("\n")[1:]:
158:         s = line.strip()
159:         if not s or s.startswith("["):
160:             continue
161:         mm = re.match(r"^(0x[0-9a-fA-F]+)$", s)
162:         return ("0x%x" % int(mm.group(1), 16)) if mm else "absent"
163:     return "absent"

166: def access_session(access_word):

168:        so a survivor has a positive observable and a corpse has none."""
169:     return session(SETUP + program([ST_W(5, 3, 0), access_word, LD_W(4, 3, 0)])
170:                    + ["print r4"])

237: #  ------------------------------------------------------------- CSR RISING EDGE
238: #
239: #  *** THIS ROW EXISTS BECAUSE THE FIRST KILL TABLE FOUND A FLOOR BREACH: M_ICSR
240: #  ESCAPED 47/47. ***  That mutant moves the accept block's UPPER bound to
241: #  CLKOFF+ICSR, making the condition `addr < 0x1ff8 || addr >= 0x1ff8' a TAUTOLOGY --
242: #  every address is accepted and the switch is NEVER REACHED, so 0x1ff8 is served as
243: #  plain RAM and the control register stops doing its work.
244: #
245: #  The rows `ok rd 1ff8' / `ok wr 1ff8' could not see it, and the reason is the one
246: #  this project keeps re-learning: THEY ARE SURVIVE-ONLY AT THAT OFFSET.  They assert
247: #  the access ran to completion, which is true on the RAM path too.  A survival oracle
248: #  cannot distinguish two arms that both survive.
249: #
250: #  THE CHANNEL THAT DOES VARY is the ICSR case's SIDE EFFECT.  Writing the READ bit
251: #  0 -> 1 calls mk48txx_update_regs(), refreshing the clock registers from the host.
252: #  So: clear ICSR, poison a clock slot, drive the rising edge, read the slot back.
253: #  Fixed build -> the poison is gone, replaced by the host value.  M_ICSR -> the
254: #  poison survives, because no update ever ran.
255: #
256: #  r5's low byte is 0x5a, and 0x5a & MK48TXX_CSR_READ (0x40) is 0x40, so ST_B of r5
257: #  BOTH poisons a slot and drives the edge -- no new instruction encoding is needed.
258: #  0x5a is a safe poison: BCD day-of-month never exceeds 0x31.
259: t, how = session(SETUP + program([ST_B(0, 2, CLKOFF + 8),      # ICSR = 0, READ bit clear
260:                                   ST_B(5, 2, CLKOFF + 13),     # poison the mday slot
261:                                   ST_B(5, 2, CLKOFF + 8),      # READ bit 0 -> 1
262:                                   LD_BU(4, 2, CLKOFF + 13)])
263:                  + ["print r4"])

269: #  *** ADDED AFTER THE PASS-2 PANEL FOUND THE SURVIVE-ONLY HOLE A SECOND TIME. ***

278: #  "every offset this detector touches -- is the DATA verified, or only survival?":
279: #
280: #      0x0100 / 0x0101   `live' / `nvram0'      DATA verified      already closed

285: #
286: #  Each row writes a DISTINCT byte, so a mutant folding several offsets onto one cell is
287: #  caught too; a single shared value would let aliasing pass.  0x5a is avoided because it
288: #  is the scratch pattern and would alias the kill rows' own observable, and 0x00 is
289: #  avoided because it is what an unwritten cell already reads.

37: #    absent      the rig deliberately has no budget (landisk: its count nearly halves under
38: #                host load, so any ceiling would be invented rather than measured)
65: # the skip code a typo in any gate was silently downgraded to "coverage gap" and run.sh
66: # still exited 0. Anything non-zero that is not 77 is a failure.
67: EXIT_SKIP=77
68: 
69: GATE_NAME=""
70: _fails=0
71: _checks=0
72: 
73: gate_begin() { GATE_NAME=$1; _fails=0; _checks=0
74:     echo "############################################################"
75:     echo "## GATE: $GATE_NAME"
76:     echo "############################################################"
77: }
78: 
79: # check <description> <actual> <expected>
80: check() {
81:     _checks=$((_checks+1))
82:     if [ "$2" = "$3" ]; then
83:         printf "  ${C_OK}ok${C_OFF}   %-52s %s\n" "$1" "$2"
84:     else
85:         printf "  ${C_BAD}FAIL${C_OFF} %-52s got=%s want=%s\n" "$1" "$2" "$3"
86:         _fails=$((_fails+1))
87:     fi
88: }
89: 
90: # check_min <description> <actual> <minimum>
91: check_min() {
92:     _checks=$((_checks+1))
93:     if [ "$2" -ge "$3" ] 2>/dev/null; then
94:         printf "  ${C_OK}ok${C_OFF}   %-52s %s (>= %s)\n" "$1" "$2" "$3"
95:     else
96:         printf "  ${C_BAD}FAIL${C_OFF} %-52s got=%s want>=%s\n" "$1" "$2" "$3"
97:         _fails=$((_fails+1))
98:     fi
99: }
100: 
101: note() { printf "  --   %s\n" "$*"; }
102: 
103: # A gate may declare that part of its coverage did not run (a rig whose image is absent,
104: # say). That must not be absorbed into a PASS: it downgrades the whole gate to SKIP, so a
105: # missing input can never be reported as evidence of correctness.
106: _degraded=0
107: degrade() { _degraded=1; printf "  ${C_SKIP}skip${C_OFF} %s\n" "$*"; }
108: 
109: gate_end() {
110:     echo
111:     if [ "$_checks" = 0 ]; then
112:         echo "${C_SKIP}$GATE_NAME: SKIP${C_OFF} (no checks ran)"; return $EXIT_SKIP
113:     elif [ "$_fails" != 0 ]; then
114:         echo "${C_BAD}$GATE_NAME: FAIL${C_OFF} ($_fails of $_checks checks)"; return 1
115:     elif [ "$_degraded" != 0 ]; then
116:         echo "${C_SKIP}$GATE_NAME: SKIP${C_OFF} ($_checks checks passed, but part of the gate could not run)"
117:         return $EXIT_SKIP
118:     else
119:         echo "${C_OK}$GATE_NAME: PASS${C_OFF} ($_checks checks)"; return 0
120:     fi
121: }
122: 
123: #  #371: gate_skip() exits with the SKIP code, which run.sh maps to
124: #  REGRESS_PASS_WITH_GAPS -- a green-ish verdict. It never consulted the running
320: # count <logfile> <pattern>   -- number of matching lines, 0 if the file is absent.
321: #
322: # NOT `[ -f x ] && grep -ac ... || echo 0`. On zero matches grep prints "0" AND exits 1,
323: # so the || arm fires too and the function emits TWO lines ("0\n0"). Measured: 3 bytes,
324: # 2 lines. That silently corrupted every marker string built from it, and made gate 6's
325: # one deliberate expected-negative assertion impossible to satisfy.
326: count() {
327:     [ -f "$1" ] || { echo 0; return; }
328:     local c
329:     c=$(grep -ac -- "$2" "$1" 2>/dev/null)
330:     echo "${c:-0}"
331: }
332: 
333: 
334: 
335:

        def visit_Name(self,node): return ast.copy_location(ast.Constant(node.id),node)
    return {n.targets[0].id:ast.literal_eval(Names().visit(n.value)) for n in ast.parse(src).body if isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and isinstance(n.targets[0],ast.Name) and n.targets[0].id in ('MANIFEST','EXEMPT')}

#!/bin/bash
#  #537 measurement: the fbpending_bound self-mutant lane.  Run under WSL Gentoo:
#     MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 wsl -d Gentoo -- bash /mnt/c/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/_scratchpad/t537/run537.sh
#  Writes ONLY under /tmp/sm537 and _scratchpad/t537/out.  Never writes GXEMUL-SEC: every arm that
#  needs a different harness runs from a SCRATCH COPY with its own selfmutant.py (the helper derives
#  SEC from its own location, so a copy is a self-contained tree).
#
#    L0  post-fix   : the working-tree harness + the gate's exact flags        want SELFMUTANT_OK
#    L1  pre-fix    : the HEAD harness (FBBOUND_VERDICT=PASS) + same flags      want SELFMUTANT_SETUP
#                     ("pristine arm did not PASS") -- the fbsmlane symptom, test-first
#    N1  blind      : post-fix harness with R3's `worst_trough >= 1' -> `>= 0'  want SELFMUTANT_FAIL
#                     (the lane must go red when the detector stops seeing the mutant)
#    N2  absolute -I: post-fix harness, -I$SEC/src/devices instead of ../     want SELFMUTANT_SETUP
#                     (both arms compile the PRISTINE copy -> binary-identity check; checks the
#                     comment gate_offline.sh now carries)
#    D0  direct     : the post-fix harness built as gate 2's main lane builds it, run WITH and
#                     WITHOUT the depth argument -- the lane runs it without one, so the
#                     no-depth IDENTITY path must be green on its own
set -u
ROOT=/mnt/c/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL
SEC=$ROOT/GXEMUL-SEC
OUT=$ROOT/_scratchpad/t537/out
S=/tmp/sm537
rm -rf "$S" "$OUT"; mkdir -p "$S" "$OUT"

OBJS=$(find "$SEC/../build/src" -name '*.o' ! -name 'main.o' ! -name 'dev_footbridge.o' 2>/dev/null | sort | tr '\n' ' ')
NOBJ=$(printf '%s' "$OBJS" | wc -w)
echo "build/ objects linked: $NOBJ" | tee "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"
[ "$NOBJ" -gt 100 ] || { echo "ABORT: too few build/ objects ($NOBJ)" | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"; exit 3; }
#  build/ freshness, recorded not asserted: the objects are what the lane links.
echo "build/gxemul mtime: $(stat -c %y "$SEC/../build/gxemul" 2>/dev/null)" | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"

XF_REL="-std=gnu99 -I../src/include -I../src/include/thirdparty -I../src/devices $OBJS -L/usr/X11R6/lib -lX11"

mkcopy() {  # dir harness-source-file -> a self-contained GXEMUL-SEC copy
    local d=$1/GXEMUL-SEC
    mkdir -p "$d/regress/selfmutants"
    cp -a "$SEC/src" "$d/src"
    #  misc.h includes "../../config.h" -- the configure-generated GXEMUL-SEC/config.h.  The helper's own
    #  mirror reaches it through its SYMLINKED src/include (the kernel resolves `..` after the link);
    #  a real copy of src/ does not, so run 1 of this script SETUP'd L1/N1 on a compile error.
    cp "$SEC/config.h" "$d/config.h"
    cp "$SEC/regress/selfmutant.py" "$d/regress/"
    cp "$SEC/regress/selfmutants/fbpending_bound.old" "$SEC/regress/selfmutants/fbpending_bound.new" "$d/regress/selfmutants/"
    cp "$2" "$d/regress/diff_fbpending_bound.c"
}

lane() {  # tag sec-root xflags
    local tag=$1 sc=$2 xf=$3 rc
    ( cd /tmp && SELFMUTANT_EXTRA_FLAGS="$xf" python3 "$sc/regress/selfmutant.py" \
        diff_fbpending_bound.c src/devices/dev_footbridge.c \
        "$sc/regress/selfmutants/fbpending_bound.old" "$sc/regress/selfmutants/fbpending_bound.new" \
        "R4 every case bounded" ) > "$OUT/$tag.txt" 2>&1
    rc=$?
    echo "$tag rc=$rc token=$(grep -oE 'SELFMUTANT_(OK|FAIL|SETUP)' "$OUT/$tag.txt" | tail -1)" | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"
    grep -E '^       ' "$OUT/$tag.txt" | sed 's/^/    /' | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"
}

#  L0 -- the REAL tree's helper and harness, exactly as gate_offline.sh's selfmutant_one calls it.
lane L0_postfix "$SEC" "$XF_REL"

#  L1 -- pre-fix: HEAD's harness in a scratch copy.
git -C "$SEC" show HEAD:regress/diff_fbpending_bound.c > "$S/harness.HEAD.c" 2>/dev/null \
    || cp "$ROOT/_scratchpad/t537/diff_fbpending_bound.HEAD.c" "$S/harness.HEAD.c"
grep -c 'FBBOUND_VERDICT=' "$S/harness.HEAD.c" | sed 's/^/HEAD harness FBBOUND_VERDICT lines: /' | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"
mkcopy "$S/pre" "$S/harness.HEAD.c"
lane L1_prefix "$S/pre/GXEMUL-SEC" "$XF_REL"

#  N1 -- the detector blinded: R3 accepts a trough of 0, so the 1 -> 0 mutant is no longer seen.
sed 's/worst_trough >= 1,/worst_trough >= 0,/' "$SEC/regress/diff_fbpending_bound.c" > "$S/harness.blind.c"
echo "N1 edit applied: $(diff "$SEC/regress/diff_fbpending_bound.c" "$S/harness.blind.c" | grep -c '^>') line(s)" | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"
mkcopy "$S/blind" "$S/harness.blind.c"
lane N1_blind "$S/blind/GXEMUL-SEC" "$XF_REL"

#  N2 -- an ABSOLUTE -I to the real tree's src/devices: both arms compile the pristine subject.
XF_ABS="-std=gnu99 -I$SEC/src/include -I$SEC/src/include/thirdparty -I$SEC/src/devices $OBJS -L/usr/X11R6/lib -lX11"
lane N2_absolute "$SEC" "$XF_ABS"

#  D0 -- the harness directly, as gate 2's main lane builds it.
( cd "$SEC/regress" && gcc -O2 -std=gnu99 -I"$SEC/src/include" -I"$SEC/src/include/thirdparty" \
    -I"$SEC/src/devices" -o "$S/fbbound" diff_fbpending_bound.c $OBJS -lm -L/usr/X11R6/lib -lX11 ) > "$OUT/D0_build.txt" 2>&1
echo "D0 build rc=$?" | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"
"$S/fbbound" 2147487744 > "$OUT/D0_deep.txt" 2>&1; echo "D0 deep rc=$?  $(grep -E '^FBBOUND_(PASS|FAIL)$|IDENTITY' "$OUT/D0_deep.txt" | tr '\n' ' ')" | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"
"$S/fbbound" > "$OUT/D0_shallow.txt" 2>&1; echo "D0 shallow rc=$?  $(grep -E '^FBBOUND_(PASS|FAIL)$|IDENTITY' "$OUT/D0_shallow.txt" | tr '\n' ' ')" | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"
echo "D0 deep: FBBOUND_VERDICT lines $(grep -c 'FBBOUND_VERDICT' "$OUT/D0_deep.txt"), ^FBBOUND_PASS\$ lines $(grep -c '^FBBOUND_PASS$' "$OUT/D0_deep.txt")" | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"

rm -rf "$S"
echo "RUN537_DONE" | tee -a "$OUT/SUMMARY.txt"

643:     #  BEHAVIOURALLY needs the UART to raise an interrupt and a guest load to
644:     #  read back which VRIP line latched, which is a rung-3 probe and a
645:     #  separate piece of work.
646:     row("S1 every UART-adding arm passes VRIP_INTR_SIU itself, no arithmetic",
647:         not no_siu, sorted(no_siu), [])
648: 
649:     #  ---- S4: the WHOLE device string, not one field of it -----------------
650:     #
651:     #  *** S1 ABOVE PINS AN ARGUMENT AND LEAVES THE FORMAT FREE, AND FIVE OF
652:     #  SEVEN PASS-2 SEATS SAID SO -- four of them producing the same class of
653:     #  escape independently. ***  The sharpest defeats S1 on its own ground:
654:     #  write the wrong line as a LITERAL IN THE FORMAT (`vrip.%i` -> `vrip.10`)
655:     #  and leave VRIP_INTR_SIU where it is.  S1 inspects the argument list, sees
656:     #  the constant passed whole, and reports "plain".  The row added
657:     #  specifically to close the wrong-but-registered-line class does not.
658:     #
659:     #  The siblings are the same hole one field over -- `addr_mult=4` ->
660:     #  `addr_mult=1` maps the UART at wrong offsets while construction still
661:     #  succeeds, and nothing here touched `addr=` either.  So this pins the
662:     #  ENTIRE string per arm rather than adding a row per field: a field-by-field
663:     #  row set would have exactly the shape that just failed, and would need
664:     #  extending every time the string gains a term.
665:     #
666:     #  It is deliberately EXACT-MATCH against a table, not a pattern.  A pattern
667:     #  is what S1 is, and the lesson of this pass is that a pattern pins what its
668:     #  author thought of.  The cost is that a legitimate change to any arm's
669:     #  device string turns this row red -- which is the intent: such a change
670:     #  should be deliberate and should update the expectation in the same edit.
671:     #
672:     #  STILL A SOURCE-TEXT ROW, with S1's honest limit unchanged: it cannot say
673:     #  the address, the multiplier or the interrupt line are right for the
674:     #  HARDWARE.  No VR41xx datasheet exists in this tree.  It says only that
675:     #  they are what this round deliberately chose, and that nothing has drifted.
676:     dev_str_bad = []
677:     for const, info in sorted(arms.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]["case_line"]):
678:         prim = (const_aliases.get(const) or [None])[0]
679:         if prim is None or not info["device_add_lines"]:
680:             continue
681:         got = device_strings(info["code"])
682:         want = EXPECT_DEVSTR.get(prim)
683:         if want is None:
684:             dev_str_bad.append((prim, "NO EXPECTATION RECORDED"))
685:         elif want not in got:
686:             dev_str_bad.append((prim, got))
687:     row("S4 each UART arm's WHOLE device string is the one recorded here",
688:         not dev_str_bad, sorted(dev_str_bad), [])
689: 
690:     #  ---- S5: the console-handle guard --------------------------------------
691:     #
692:     #  *** S4 CLOSED THREE OF THE FOUR MUTANTS FIVE PASS-2 SEATS PRODUCED; THIS
693:     #  IS THE FOURTH, AND IT ESCAPED S4 BECAUSE IT IS NOT IN THE DEVICE STRING.
694:     #  ***  Delete the guard TEST and leave the assignment:
695:     #
696:     #      if (!machine->x11_md.in_use)          ->      if (1)
697:     #              machine->main_console_handle = x;
698:     #
699:     #  Measured: 27/27, every other row green.  Under X11 the ns16550 would then
700:     #  take the console handle that dev_vr41xx_init's KIU console is supposed to
701:     #  hold -- which is precisely the behaviour the #444 reorder was written to
702:     #  PRESERVE, since dev_vr41xx_init used to win by running last.
703:     #
704:     #  It cannot be caught behaviourally here: THIS PROBE NEVER EXERCISES X11,
705:     #  and a headless run cannot.  A pass-2 seat named that gap independently
706:     #  before naming the mutant.  So this is a source-text row like S1 and S4,
707:     #  and it is labelled one.
708:     #
709:     #  It pins the GUARDED SHAPE rather than an exact block, because the three

p=pathlib.Path('_scratchpad/p2_536_537/frozen/check_probe_wiring.staged.py')

class Names(ast.NodeTransformer):
    def visit_Name(self,node): return ast.copy_location(ast.Constant(node.id),node)
tables={n.targets[0].id:ast.literal_eval(Names().visit(n.value)) for n in t.body if isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and isinstance(n.targets[0],ast.Name) and n.targets[0].id in ('MANIFEST','EXEMPT')}

    class path:
        @staticmethod
        def isdir(p): return True
        @staticmethod
        def basename(p): return 'regress'
        @staticmethod
        def abspath(p): return p
        @staticmethod
        def join(*p): return '/'.join(p)
def run(probe_date, inclusive=False, replace_dates=None):
    manifest=tables['MANIFEST']
    exempt=dict(tables['EXEMPT'])
    if replace_dates: exempt.update(replace_dates)
    sites={n:['fixture.sh:1'] for n,v in manifest.items() if v[0]=='CLASS_DETECTOR' and n not in exempt}
    env={'DEFAULT_REGRESS':'regress','os':FakeOS,'gate_list':lambda p:(['fixture.sh'],None),'ALWAYS':[], 'exec_sites':lambda p,s:(sites,[],[]), 'domain':lambda p:(sorted(manifest),'in-memory fixture'), 'MANIFEST':manifest,'EXEMPT':exempt,'CLASSES':('CLASS_DETECTOR','CLASS_WITNESS','CLASS_TOOL','CLASS_RESEARCH'), 'CLASS_DETECTOR':'CLASS_DETECTOR','CLASS_WITNESS':'CLASS_WITNESS','CLASS_TOOL':'CLASS_TOOL','DATE_RE':re.compile(r'"'^'"\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}"'$'"'),'self_label':lambda p:None}
    body=ast.get_source_segment(src,main)
    if inclusive:
        assert body.count('elif d < today:')==1
        body=body.replace('elif d < today:','elif d <= today:')
    exec(compile(body,'<isolated-main>','exec'),env)
    buf=io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf): rc=env['main'](['--today',probe_date])
    out=buf.getvalue()
    return rc, sum('EXPIRED EXEMPTION' in x for x in out.splitlines())

2347: def gate_list(regress):
2348:     """The battery's OWN array is the authority on which scripts run.
2349: 
2350:     Deriving from run.sh rather than from a gate_*.sh glob is deliberate, and carries a
2351:     second-order property worth having: a gate script that exists but was dropped out of

GO-WITH-CHANGES

Add an expiry test **on the stamped date**, and make missing build objects produce an explicit, consistent gate status. The required full-gate runs remain outstanding.

For Q1:

- **P1: correct for the stated include layout.** The helper creates a real `<mirror>/regress` directory and compiles there. Symlinking the harness does not change that working directory or redirect `-I../src/devices` into the original tree. After the unsuccessful earlier searches, that option selects the mirror’s subject. This follows [GCC’s documented include search order](https://gcc.gnu.org/onlinedocs/cpp/Search-Path.html); the supplied L0/N2 results corroborate it.
- **P2: correct.** `build_and_run()` invokes `[binp]`, without arguments. The shallow count enumerates as R0: 1; R1/R5/R3: 7 each; R6/R6b: 2; SELFCHECK verdict: 1; R4: 1; IDENTITY: 1—**27**. R2 adds one, giving **28**. The two deliberate SELFCHECK rows are printed but excluded from those counters, so the gate’s raw row grep sees **29/30**, respectively. The supplied mutant result has seven R3 failures, sufficient to redden R4 without R2.
- **P3: incomplete as an equivalence claim.** The helper also adds `-I../src`, changes link-input ordering, and hardcodes `gcc`; [gate 2 selects `cc` before `gcc`](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/_scratchpad/p2_536_537/frozen/gate_offline.sh:185). The measurement driver also hardcodes `gcc`, so D0 does not establish compiler identity with the actual gate. The listed flags do not inherently invalidate the counter assertions, and the supplied results support this toolchain, but they do not establish general equivalence. The missing measurement is compiler identity plus a mutant run using the main lane’s actual compiler and flags. WSL denied my read-only compiler-version queries.
- **P4: locally correct, but incomplete for the whole gate.** Assuming earlier prerequisites let execution reach these sections, the new lane fails setup while the main section merely notes a skip. However, the later `SELFCHECK manifest: every COVERED detector ran its sentinel` also requires `diff_fbpending_bound.log`. An absent log fails; a retained previous log can satisfy it because the no-object branch does not regenerate it. Do not copy the note-only skip. If buildless operation is supported, use the existing [`degrade()` mechanism](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/lib.sh:103), which makes the gate return SKIP/77, and coordinate both lanes and sentinel accounting without accepting stale logs.
- **P5: correct on a valid baseline.** Strictly, expiry evaluates **present, unwired DETECTORs**. Stale exemptions and missing files already make the baseline invalid. The [selftest guard](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/_scratchpad/p2_536_537/frozen/check_probe_wiring.staged.py:2731) does check every MANIFEST path before running cases, and every manifest key is a `.py` path. `ieee_store:2099-01-01` belongs to the separate shell self-mutant exemption table; it is outside M5’s population.

For Q2, these are predictions from the code, assuming otherwise-green prerequisites. “Precommit” below means the supplied wiring checker and its selftest; other hook checks were outside the permitted reading scope. The two gate-2 failure cases both redden **`selfmutant fbpending_bound: the detector still detects, via its named row`**.

| Weakening | Gate 2 | gate_arm | Wiring precommit | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **(a) Unconditional `FBBOUND_PASS`** | **FAIL** | PASS | PASS | The mutant lacks a FAIL token. Helper returns `SELFMUTANT_FAIL`, even though R4 prints FAIL. |
| **(b) Grep `FBBOUND_`** | **PASS** | PASS | PASS | No check detects this weakening on healthy input. The main verdict check now accepts `FBBOUND_FAIL`; a deep-only failure can evade the shallow self-mutant lane, while the remaining main checks require only R2’s presence and a row-count floor. |
| **(c) Include `dev_footbridge.o`** | **FAIL**, with that object present | PASS | PASS | Expected duplicate external definitions from the included translation unit and object produce pristine-link SETUP. Garbage collection is not permission for duplicate definitions; [GNU ld normally rejects them](https://sourceware.org/binutils/docs/ld/Options.html#index-_002d_002dallow_002dmultiple_002ddefinition). If the object is absent, removing the exclusion changes nothing. |
| **(d) R4 uses `failures <= 1`** | **PASS** | PASS | PASS | Pristine has zero failures; the committed mutant has seven. Both still satisfy their expected outcomes. This mutant does not test R4’s one-failure boundary. The overall harness verdict still rejects one failure. |
| **(e) Remove `"$PMAX"`** | PASS | **FAIL** | PASS | The command still contains **`gate`**, which becomes `sys.argv[1]`, the executable name. The question’s “no binary argument” premise is false. With no executable named `gate`, the probe raises before its sentinel; **`mk48 nvram probe completed`** fails. The wiring scanner does not validate arguments. |
| **(f) M5b uses latest date** | PASS | PASS | **PASS** | All eleven dates are identical today. Even with staggered dates, changing **only M5b** to latest-plus-one still produces an expired exemption and satisfies its generic assertion. If the shared date also changes M5a, sufficiently separated dates can instead redden M5a. |

The most consequential additional selftest gap is at [M5a/M5b](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/_scratchpad/p2_536_537/frozen/check_probe_wiring.staged.py:2825). I executed the checker’s `main()` in memory with controlled inventory and wiring inputs:

| Simulated date | Current `<` comparison | Incorrect `<=` comparison |
|---|---|---|
| 2026-09-26 | PASS | PASS |
| 2026-09-27 | PASS | FAIL: eleven expired |
| 2026-09-28 | FAIL: eleven expired | FAIL: eleven expired |

Thus both existing M5 cases accept the incorrect boundary. Add an **on-date PASS** assertion. A fixture with two distinct deadlines should also assert which exemption expires first; otherwise Q2(f) remains invisible.

For Q3, the option sets are incomplete:

- **#537:** teach the helper an explicit legacy verdict format, or parameterize its verdict parser. Renaming and printing two tokens are not the only choices. The house-token rename remains reasonable.
- **#536:** stagger deadlines, or retire a demonstrably superseded detector with documented replacement coverage. Neither is equivalent to silently relabeling it WITNESS. These alternatives do not override the owner’s ruling.
- **M5:** use a synthetic exempt detector. That preserves expiry testing even when the production exemption table becomes empty, instead of making successful debt removal break the selftest.

Two further findings under the standing licence:

Fixing that machine would fail this newly gated probe. Make that classifier control independent of a live unrelated defect; F3 already provides a synthetic unrelated-abort check.
- The new checker comment saying **“BOTH HALVES OF THAT PAIRING ARE GONE”** is false. The pairing described immediately above concerns #442’s debts; `fbpending_drain_probe.py` remains exempt. Correct that claim.
